KA JINGLAMPHRANG 


Nga dap da ka jingsngewnguh kaba khraw eh ia U Blei TrAt 
Kynrad Nongbuh Nongthaw Uba la pile ia nga kum kane ka lad 
ban lum ban lang ia u kew uba dangsah ryngkhai hangne hangtai 
bad ban bub shi jaka ha kane ka thiar jong nga. Da kaba eh abi-

bun bad shim per stem ban pyndap thiar bad ban tai ia ka jinglong 
tynrai jong ka jaid bynriew Khasi Kbara bad sbaphang ka jingkrin 
jaka shane shatai hapoh la ka Ri, ki kam shlur jong ki riewkhlain 
bad ki jingjia bapbylla bym pat ju iohsngew ha kaba lei kjM tymmen 
jong ngi ki la leh ban saindur bad sengnongrim ia ka Ri bad jaid 
bynriew naduh hyndai hynthai ha ka por bym pat don jingthoh jing- 
tar ei ei. Lah ban ong ba hadien ka jinglaitluid jong ka Ri India, 
ka la don ka jingkyndit bynriew ha ka thoh ka tar Khasi bad kaba 
la pynkhih im thymmai ia ka jingmut jong ki Sarnia Nongthoh Kha- 
si ban thoh sbaphang ki riti ki dustur, ka jingioh jingkot, ka kamai 
ka kajih, ka khaii-pateng, ka synshar ka khadar bad ki niam ki ru- 
kom ; bynrei bun napdeng jong ki ki iaid lait noh na ka Hyndai ba 
kaba ki Pakulong kumah jong ngi, ki la suit la shor bad buh maw- 
nongrim ha la ki shnong ki shnong bad ha la ki bima sima. 


Ka jingpyrshang barit jong nga ban lum ban lang na ki saw- 
dong pud ka Ri ia ki thymmei jong kane ka Ri baieit jong ngi kaba 
ki ong. “Ka Ri Tipbriew Tipblei, Ka Ri shongpdeng pyrthei, Ka 
Ri baieit ki blei”. Nga shem ba ka la don lypa la ka jong ka histo- 
ry, ka geography bad ka philosophy bad ka la don ruh ka Riti 
Synshar paitbah kaba kynsai tarn ha ka pyrthei naduh hyndai byn- 
thai. Ka long ka Ri kaba dap da ki jingphylla suda, ki jingjia ba 
maian, ki kam shlur jong ki kpa tymmen na mynhyndai kiba ym 
don thylliej ban batai bad lada la pynmih pyrthei ia ki, ka pyrthei 
hi kan lyngngoh ngain. Kim sbym dei ki puriskam kai lane ki pu- 
rinam ba sbu kren pnah thylliej manore ; faynrei ki long ki jingsbi- 
sha (authentic) kiba da pynsah jingkjrnmaw ruh pateng la paten^ 
bynriew kiba long kum ki tusbir bad kiba da pynleit ha ki niam ki 
rukom bad ki riti ki dustur. Kumta, nga pynshlur ia ki Samla ka 
Ri kiba don sap ban tih ia ki umpohliew jingstad jong kane ka U 
baieit jong ngi. Ngim donkam ia ki Mynderi ban wan batai stad ia 
ki riti ki dustur bad ia ka jinglong sotti jong kane ka Ri jong ngi. 
Hynrei dei ma ngi ki ban iathuh ba ka pyrthei ba ngi long ki Trai- 
muluk ki Traijaka bad ki jaid bynriew bapher (distinct races) ba ka

pyrthei. 


Mynta, baba la dep lum dep lang bad sdang ban pynmih 
thei sbaphang ki jingjia jong ka Ri bad ka jaid bynriew jong ngi ha 
kane ka thiar, ngam sngewphylla ban ong ba don hangne hangtai hi 
jingbakla badjingduna kiba bun. Namarkata, nga kyr^d ia ki 
Nongpule ban pdiang sngewbha bad ruh ban kdew ia ki jingduna. 



Da ka jingbymbanse eh ban kten shlur hangnc, ba ba kane ka 
juk wad jingstad (Research work) baroh baroh ki Mynderi ki la 
wan ban shu jler bad batai stad ia ka jaidbynriew Khasi Khara, ia 
ki riti ki dustur bad jinglong sotti jong kane ka Ri jong ngi khlem 
da salia ban iaiam bakia ia u paidtrap paidknup bashida mynsiem 
tang ban kamai pisa. Haba phai khamtam sha Ki War, ki riewstad 
na ri Nongwei ki la shu phlan duman khlem da tip ia ki jingshisha 
ba la suit ba la shor da ki Longkni ki Longkpa, kumta, ka la pynlong 
ia ki War ban lung khapnap (conservative) bad byin kwah iano iano 
ban tip ia ki jinglong jong ki bad ia ki jingtboh jingtar barim bajah 
(Old documents) kiba don ha ki iing ki sem jong ki lane ha ki Ka* 
chad (Courts). Kumta, kum u bricw uba la kha la pun sha Ri 
War, nga la pyrshang katba kot synei la i bor i buit barit jong nga 
ban ia syllok bad katto katne ki lok ki jor kiba dangtip tymmcn bad 
na ki kot ki sla (Old documents) kiba dangsah ryngkbai hangne 
hangtai ; hynrei wat la katta ruh kirn pat biang satia bad dang bun 
ki jingduna. 

Kawei kaba sngewsih ba ki riewstad ba mynta, ki kwah bha ban 
wad jingtip (Research) shaphang ki riti ki dustur bad ka synshar ka 
kbadar jong ki War naduh hyndai hynthai khamtam eh ia ka Hima 
Sheila kaba donnam kaba la pynmih bun ki riewstad bad ki 
riewproh jabieng. Ki riewstad ba mynta, ki da pyni 
ruh ia la ka iktiar bad ka bor jingstad jabieng jong ki da kaba shu

pda kharei bad batai stad ia ki jingmut tynrai jong ki hima sima 
lane ki Wadar Sheila. Ka jingmut jong ka ktien *Wadar’ kumba 
kynnoh ki War lane ‘Wahadadar’ kumba kynnoh ki phareng, ki 
riewstad ba mynta ki ong, ba katei ka ktien ka wan na ka ktien Per- 
sia, “Diddar" kaba mut hi u Nongsynshar. U Sushil Chandara 
Dutta ha ka kot jong u, “The North East and the Mughals”, 
P. 219 u ong, “The Chief of the Sheila areas took the title of Waba- 
dadars which was of Persian origin,” bad u la ong shuh shuh, 
“These Officials were elected for three years each by the people.” 
Kaba sngewphylla shibun eh ban ong ba une u Nongthoh u la pyn- 
kylla dustur hi marwei ia ka dustur Sheila da kaba ong ba ka jing- 
jied ia ki Wadar da u paitbah ka long sbisien man ka lai-snem 
katba ha ka dustur Sheila pat ka jinglong Wadar naduh hyndai 
hynthai ka long shirta-jingim. Sa kawei pat kajingong, ba ka 
ktien Wahadadar ka mih na ka ktien Persia. Katba ia tip lang sha 
Ri War, ki Sheila kirn ju shah synshar mynno mynno hapoh ki 
Mongol lane ha ki nong Persia. Hynrei ka ktien Wadar lane Wa- 
hadadar ka long ka jingmut tynrai jong ka ktien Sheila hi kaba wan 
na ka ktien, “Duwakdar” kaba mut ka jingshai ka la wan. Kane 
ka la mih na ka shyntur jong U Jin Kyllut, uba la long u Statesman 
bad u Philosopher jong ka Hima Sheila ha kata ka por, uba ki ai 
nam ia u, “U Aristotle jong ka Ri Mihngi.” 

Nangta, la mih sa u Dr. J. B. Bhattacherjoe, Department of 



(iii) 

History, North Eastern Hill University, uba la thoh ha ka Intro*- 
duction jong ka “Report on the Khasi & Jaintia Hills, 1853 by 
A. J.M. Mills da kaba ong kumne — “Guha Singh, the Chief of 
Sheila, died in British captivity in Murshidabad in 1792” (P. xv). 
Kumba ngi tip u syiem Sheila u dei u Ganga Singh b^d um dei sa- 
tia u Guha Singh. U Ganga Singh u dei tang a syiem mravr uba

shongdor tang 23 pen (bdi) ba la leit thied da ki Shelia na Mymen- 
sing ban synshar shipor katba dang don ki kynrum kynram ha ka 
Hima sima bad une u la synshar ia ka Hima 23 sbnong tang 6 snem. 
la u la pyniap hi da ki riewthma jong ka Hima 2i shnong ryngkat 
bad ka jingiarap Jong ki riewthma Jyrwa kiba na Nonglwai. U 
Ganga Singh um pat Ju iaieh thma bad ki bar- Hima lane bad ki 
Phareng namar ba u la iap shuwa katei ka snem ba la buh da u 
Bhattacherjee. Kane ka jingleh stad ki Mynderi na NEHU ka la 
pynsniewdur ia ki riti ki dustur jong uba rim uba jah bad la ialam 
bakla ia kiba pli ki Nongpule. 

Hangne nga pynpaw ia ka jingsngewnguh jong nga ia I Pa, I 
Mr. Khriwell Roy Lona, iba la pynsan pynrangbah ia nga bad iba 
la ai mynsiem bad jingialam jingmut ia nga ban thoh shaphang ka 
Hyndai. Kumjuh ruh ia i Parad Rahin Lona, Parad Kclon Dieng* 
doh, Meirad Nesi Cbyne, Parad Kodlin langrai kiba la khlad nob 
baroh kiba na ka Hima Mawlong da ka jingaibuit ai mynsiem jong 
ki shaphang ka Hima Mawlong naduh hyndai. Nangta, da kaba 
sngewnguh eh, nga ai khublei ia ki Rangbah jong ka Sheila Confe- 
deracy kum ki jong 1 Kni Rangbah jong ngi, I Mr. Kriso Mobon 
Roy Chyne, ba la khlad noh, Ex-Acting Wadar, nangta, 1 Mr. 
Gonopati Shongwan, Mr. Seibor Owen Sing Chyne, Ex-Asstt. Tea- 
cher. Mr. E. Tenderly Chyne, Ex- Wadar, Shelia Confederacy, Mr. 
E. Weston Dkhar, Nongwar (Receipient of the State Award in Lite- 
rature) kiba la iarap ia nga ha ki bynta baroh. Da ka jingsngew- 
nguh ruh ia ki Rangbah bad Longkmie na ka Hima Sohbar kum ki 
jong i Mr. Rostanwell War, Ex-Sirdar Hima Sohbar bad Ex-M.O.C. 
District Council, United Khasi & Jaintia Hills Shillong, Mr. Jendro 
Maliaw, Mr. Horder Ryngnga, Mrs. Phebon Ryngnga, Mrs. Kron 
Shullai, ba la khlad noh, kumjuh ruh i Dr. Opren Sing Khongsit 
bad Mr. Skilful Ryngnga kiba la ai jingtipshibun ia nga shaphang ka

juh ruh ia i Mr. Wisely Ryngnga na Umniuh bad Mr. Drawingson

Dkhar na Nongshken, iba long kum u Adviser, Raid Non^nken 
Welfare Association kiba la ai jingtip ia nga shaphang la ki jong ki

Kumjuh ruh ia i Prof. Siprian Cbyne (Bam kwai ha ling U BleO iba

SHIKYNTIEN 

Nga la ioh ban pule ia ka kot Na Ki Hamsaia Ka Myanor 
ia kaba la thoh da 1 Bah E. W. Chyne. 1 Bah Estodarwell i del iba 
la pyllut ia la ka por ha kaba wad bniah shaphang kiei kiba la jia 
ha ka mynnor ha kane ka ri jong ngi khamtam ha ki thain ri War. 
Ha kane ka kot ruh I la pynpaw ia la ka sap ha kaba buh beit buh 
ryntih ha ka rukom ki khanapateng kat kum ka jingshisha katba 
lah ban lum. I la pynpaw ruh ia ka jingduna jong ki nongthohkot 
khamtam kito kiba nabar jong ka ri jong ngi ba ki kum kynthob ia 
kici kiei ki bym sbisha. Kane barob ka lah ban long namar ba kito 
kiba nabar ri haba ki thoh ci ei shaphang ka ri jong ngi kim shym 
la lah satia ban wad bniah kum ki nongthoh trai ri. Teng teng wat 
ki nongthoh trai ri hi ruh ki kum thoh ia kici kiei kiba ki shim, ba

ki long kiba shisha katba ynda haba la wad bniah shuh sbuh lah 
ban shem ba donkam ban pynbeit ia ki jingthoh ba mynshwa namar 
ba la pynshong nongrim tang ha ki katto katne ki jingshisha khlem 
da ioh lut ia ki jingshisha baroh. 

Ha kane ka kot kaba kynthup ia ki 10 lynnong la thoh lut ia 
ka jin^oi ki Hima Khasi ha ki thain ri War. Bun na kine kiba ngi 
la ioh jingtip raalu mala kum U Lei Shyllong, ka Hima Shyllong 
bad ka Pam Shala. Hynrei ha nga hi ka long kaba thymmai ban 
tip shaphang ka Ar-Hima jong u Syiemlieh bad Syiemiong ba ki 
thain Ri War tad haduh ba la pynlong ia ka Hima Sohra. La thoh 
bniah shaphang kitei ki Hima ba la jer haneng bad ruh shaphang 
ki Hima Sohbar bad Mawlong de. Ha ka jingshisha katba nga 
kynmaw une u juh u nongthoh u la thoh ia kawei ka kot hapdeng 
ki snem 1977 shaphang tang ka Hima Mawlong. 

Ka jingthoh shaphang ka Hima Sheila lane ka Hima Arphew 
Lai shnong ruh ka long kaei kaei kaba ai jingmut shibun shaphang 
jong ki nong seng ia kane ka Hima, khamtam shaphang u Jin Kyi* 
lut, uba long da shisha u riewstad pyrkbat ha ka kam sain hima 
sima da kaba peit ia ki sawdong sawkun jong ka pyrtbei bad ia ki 
saw aiom jong ka mariang ban pynwandur ia ka synshar khadar da 
ki saw Duwakdar. Ka jingthoh shaphang u syiem Ganga Singh 
jong ka Hima Sheila ka long kaei kaei kaba pyni shai kdar tha* 



(Vi) 

phang ka jingiapher ka rukom pyrkhat hapdeng ki Kbasi bad ki 
Dkhar. Katba ki Sheila ki shim ia u ba u dei tang u syiem mraw 
jong ki» u Ganga Singh pat u shim ialade ba u dei u syiem lah duh 
bad sa poi sha kiei kiei ki jingjia kiba sngewsih hapoh ka hima 
Sima. Toi, ym don syiem shuh hapoh ka Hima Sheila naduh kata 
ka por. Ka rukom pyrkhat U Jin Kyllut ka iai sah ka nongrim

synshar khadar hapoh ka Hima Sheila tad haduh ba ki bor District 
Council ki la shu shaniah noh tang ha uwei u Duwakdar. 


Ka jingiathuhkhana shaphang ka Bthuh bad ki khun jong ka 
ruh ka long kaba sngewtynnad bad kaba iathuh iangi shaphang ka 
jingkhlain bor met jong ki rangbah Khasi hyndai. Teng teng kane 
ka jingkhlain bor met jong ki ka la kylla long noh kaei kaei kaba 
ngim lah ban pei phang ha kine ki por kiew dieng sohmynken 
mynta. Kumjuh ruh shaphang ka khaii pateng hapdeng ki Khasi 
bad ki Dkhar u nongthoh u la lah ban batai ia ka jinglong jong ka 
ioh ka kot jong kito kiba shong ha ri War, ka ri kaba long da shi- 
sha kaba riewspah bad ka ri jong u soh u pai ha kito ki por kiba 
mynshwa. Ka lynnong kaba ichadduh shaphang ka jinghiarthma u 
Mir Jumla ruh ka la pynpaw shaphang ka jingnang jingstad jong ki 
Khasi ha ki kam ialeh thma. Wat haba la poi ha kano kano ka 
khim, U Blei U dang ri synei ia ka ri tipbriew tipblei jong kato ka 
por. 


Na ki Hamsala Ka Mynnor ka la long da shisha ka kot kaba 
ai jingtip ia ngi khambun shuh shuh shaphang ki Hima ka ri War. 
Ka ai jingtip ruh ia ngi shaphang ki briew kiba shong ha ka kiba la 
pyntbit ialade ha ki kam sain hima sima, ha ka ioh ka kot, ha ka 
thma ka ktien, bad ka jingiadei hapdeng para briew. Nga ai khu- 
blei iaroh ia u nongthoh na ka bynta kane ka kot ha kaba u la iai 
minot ban trei haduh ba kan da paw pyrthei. Nga kyrmen ba ki 
nongpule kin shem ba ka long kaba ai jingmyntoi shibun. Nga 
khmihlynti ba u nongthoh u nang iai pynmih shuh shuh ia kiba kum 
kine ki jingthoh. Nga kyrmen ruh ba un nang iaithoh kham bniah 
shaphang kiei kiei ia kiba u la thoh ha kane ka kot, kum shaphang 
ka Ar-Hima bad kiwei kiwei de. 


B. Pakein.

Mr. B. C. Jyrwa, 

Head, 

Dcptt. of Khasi 
Shillong College, Shillong.

The 30th. Aug. 1995. 


KA MYNNOR KA DON LA KA DOR 

Naduh ba ngi dang iahiar iakiew na ka jingkiengkstar u khun 
Khasi Khara, u la don la ka jong ka kticn, iakabala pynkhamtt 
da U Blei Trai Kynrad. Naduh kata ka por, ka ktien hok ka ktiea 
sot ka siang lynti ia ki $ha ka sain kur ka sain kha, ka sain Riti ka 
sain Hima. U da pynieng ruh ia ka daw bad pyneh la ka rngiew 
ka rwiang, ban neh ban sah ka longbriew manbriew, ha kane ka 
pyrthei. Hadien kata u scng sa ka Niam ka rukom kaba da ka hu- 
kum. Nangta u sain dur sa ia ka akor ka buroin, ban sneng ban 
kraw la ki para ki pyrsa, kumno ba kin im donburom, shongdonbti* 
rom, sah donburom, ba ka longbriew manbriew jong ki kan phuh 
kan phieng, ba ka longing longsem ruh kan shongkun shongblei. 
Nangta shuh shuh ki seng sa ka longkha ka longman ne ka longkha 
ka longkur ba kin iar la u tnum u tyndai. Ynda ki la iaroi iapa 
pat, ki seng sa ki shnong ki Raid ki Hima. Hadien kata, ki sa sain 
sa ia ka hok ka sot ka ban neh pateng la pateng. 

Dei halor kine ki .Tingshisha, ba ki sa pynskhem la u pud u 
sam, ba kin bad burom ia la ka jong, bad lada jia ba ki non^ei ne

ki nongshun ki wan tur ruma, ngi sngcwthuh shai ba lada ki kren 
tang shikyntien ne pyllait tang sbikhnam, u nam blei na ka ryng- 
kap, ki nongtabpud ki nongtabsam kin kylla mulct. Haba ngi 
bishar sa na ka liang ka duwai ka phirat, ka knia ka khriam, ka 
shat ka khcin, kajerkathoh bad ka khan ka khad, ngishem ba 
ym don tbylliej ban batai ia ka jingmaian jong ki. 

Haba ki saindur sa ia ka rongbiria, ka ksaw ka kpong, ka dud 
sabuit ka dud skai, ne haba pyrsad ia ka ingding, haba tab ia ka 
mynsaw mynsnam, ne baba dwai ba phirat ban pyntbanda ia ka 
shit ka syang, haba wan jngoh ki blei lyngba u Niangpyrsit, tang 
da ka thang syrdep, ka jem jai kam ju thynrei. 

Hato kiba batai stad ia ka Riti ka dustur jong ngi ki nud ma 
ban khyllie niuhmat halor kine ki jingshisha baroh ? Hato ki tip 
ia ka bynta (ka role) jong ka khun khatduh ba ka Khasi Society ? 
Tang namar ba ki la sngewstad, ki thap pynbakla wat ia ka tynrai 
ruh. Hynrei nga ong shai bangne ba katba dangsah u Lum Shyl- 
long, katba ym pat ngam ka Ri Khasi ym don ba lah ban tbombor 
ia kane ka Ri baieit ki blei. 

Nalor nangta, nga ong shuh shuh hangne— katba dang don ki 
thei saitsobphlang, ki the! kheitpheinani, ki thei shaagltttQ 
shangwab, ki thei tongshersyngkai lane katba dang doiikt Rang ieit* 



(viii) 


thiah khynraw, ki Rang Sumnamlang, ki Rang iabehmrad, ki Rang 
khalaiwait, ki Rangbat-khnam ha lympung ka rongbiria, Ki Rang-

KA HIMA SOHBAR 

Hyndai ki ong ki War baroh ki dei ki nongshong shnong jong 
ka Hima bhyllong bakhiaw hynrei namar ki jingiakhih bad jingia- 
umsnam ha ka hima ki la hiar noh sha Ri War. Ka Hima Sohbar 
ka la mih ryngkat bad ka Hima Mawlong, Shelia bad kiwei kiwei ki 
hima rit ba la synshar da ki Sordar, Lyngdob lane Basan. Ka Hima 
Sohbar ka la long kawei na ki Hima Khasi kaba khlain tarn ha ki 
thain Ri-War-Mihngi ha kata ka por. Ki pud ki sam jong ka na ki 
phang mihngi, ka ia kynduh bad u Syiem Synteng, kata naduh 
Mawsiang-shyngliar bad ki Umniuh, shatei la kerpud da u Syiem 
Khyrini bad na shaphang shathie haduh Sylhct District. La ong ba 
shuwa ban mih ka Hima Sohra bad Mawsmai, ki pud ki sam jong 
ka ki poi shaduh Saitsohpen markhap pud bad ka hima Kyntur- 
sniang. Ma shaphang sepngi ka poi shaduh Mawmluh kajaka u 
Syiemlieh bad haduh Umri-umpur bad Wahutim ka hima jong u 
Syicmiong. Ki shnong kiba kynthup hapoh kane ka hima ki 
long — (1) Ka Nongshken, (2) Umniuh, (3) Nongjri, i4) Jyrkaw, 
(5) Tangwai, (6) Wahlong, (7) Tharia, (8) Komora, (9) Mawkyn- 
teng, (10) Laittyra, (II) Mawsmai, (12) Mawpun, (13) Umdud, 
(14) Nongrum, (15) Nongneng, (16) Nongskong (17) Saitsohpen. 
Ki shnong ri dkhar ki kynthup ia ka — (1) Chattak, (2) Talikhar, 
(3) Kulairak bad (4) Pandua kaba iadei pud bad u Syiem Synteng. 
Ki tymmen ki iathuh ha ka por ba ki Sohbar ki dang don ha Bhoi 
Umsohbar, ia ka hima jong ki la synshar da uwei u Syiem uba kyr- 
teng u Jawunibar. Don kiba ong ba ka Pahsyntiew ka dei ka khun 
jong u Syiem Jawumbar kaba kem puri kaba la phet noh na la iing 
bad ieit shong ha krem Marai.

Ka Thma Bad U Hindu Raja : Ha kata ka por ladon uwei 
u Syiem ri dkbar uba la tip ha Mawlong kum U Hindu Raja. 
U long u syiem uba khraw bad uba pawnam, uba don u pud u

shnong. Kat ka burom bad ka jingkbraw jong une u syiem hynrei

Ki Kam Radbah U Ramhah Ha Ri War Bad Ri Lm Khasi t 

Ka Bthuh bapli ynda la iap nob ki khun Rangbah jong ka U Bnang 
bad U Wein ba Ri War, ka la sngewkynjab bad sngewkhuslai ba la 
marwei hi hapdeng ka jinglong tymmen. Kunta, ka la leit par sa
